Job description
About the Team

The Strategic Finance team provides financial insights and guidance to support OpenAI’s long-term goals and strategies. We partner across the business to allocate and deploy our resources for the highest-impact outcomes.  ￼

Within Strategic Finance, the B2B team focuses on the financial performance of our products and GTM functions, ensuring tight alignment between financial objectives and company strategy. We partner with leaders across Product, GTM, Research, Partnerships, and Operations to:

 - Drive operational planning, financial forecasting, and performance management.

 - Provide decision-quality insights on product and financial performance to inform strategic resource allocation.

 - Build the “0→1” financial foundations required to scale and accelerate growth.

About the Role

We are hiring a senior leader in B2B Strategic Finance to build and scale a new pillar within our finance organization. This is a highly visible role that reports into the Head of B2B Strategic Finance and supports some of our most critical executive stakeholders, including our COO, CFO, and CRO, among others on the B2B Leadership Team.

This role is ideally based in our San Francisco HQ, but we are open to NYC and Seattle. We use a hybrid work model of 3 days in the office per week and offer relocation assistance to new employees.

In this role, you will:

Drive B2B finance scale and rigor

 - Build and scale core financial infrastructure across the B2B business, including forecasting methodology, variance management, and performance narratives that drive accountability and decision-making velocity.

 - Lead consolidated planning across revenue, gross margin (including compute), and opex for annual budget, forecasts, regular business reviews, and long-range planning.

 - Establish durable management reporting: KPI definitions, dashboards, month-end/quarter-end deliverables, and exec-ready readouts.

 - Partner with Corporate FP&A, Accounting, and Finance Systems/Data to evolve processes and controls toward public-company standards.

Be a strategic partner to B2B leadership

 - Lead forecasting and business reviews across usage, revenue, and gross margin, including scenario modeling tied to product & engineering constraints and compute economics.

 - Holistically manage the financial performance and unit economics of our B2B products.

 - Partner with Compute and Engineering on margin optimization and quantifying trade-offs across   product experience, cost, and scalability.

Stand up emerging high-stakes, strategic initiatives

 - Support high-priority strategic projects from 0 →1, partnering closely with leadership and adjacent B2B finance teams to stand up the initial financial frameworks, decision support, and performance tracking.

 - Translate complex problems into clear executive narratives (trade-offs, risk/return, and strategic recommendations).

 - Transition emerging initiatives to long-term owners as they scale.

You might thrive in this role if you have:

 - 12+ years of progressive experience in Strategic Finance, Product Finance, Corporate FP&A, Corporate Development, Investment Banking/PE/VC, or Management Consulting—plus meaningful operating experience in a fast-scaling technology company.

 - Deep familiarity with B2B subscription and consumption / usage-based business models and the ability to reason fluently about unit economics and margin drivers (including compute-driven COGS).

 - Expert modeling and analytical skills (multi-scenario P&L and cash flow modeling, sensitivity analysis, and strong financial judgment).

 - A strong ability to critically evaluate opportunities and risks in financials; attention to detail and accuracy in financial reporting, budgeting, and analysis.

 - Demonstrated ability to bring structure to ambiguity, lead senior stakeholders through trade-offs, and drive crisp decisions in high-uncertainty environments.

 - Exceptional communication and executive presence—strong at building Board/exec-ready narratives from complex, cross-functional inputs.

 - A “roll up your sleeves” mindset, high ownership, and deep curiosity about AI and how it will reshape enterprises, industries, and institutions.

 - Experience with SQL or a willingness to learn.

 - A passion for helping build world-class finance teams and driving business and financial outcomes.



About OpenAI

OpenAI is an AI research and deployment company dedicated to ensuring that general-purpose artificial intelligence benefits all of humanity. We push the boundaries of the capabilities of AI systems and seek to safely deploy them to the world through our products. AI is an extremely powerful tool that must be created with safety and human needs at its core, and to achieve our mission, we must encompass and value the many different perspectives, voices, and experiences that form the full spectrum of humanity.

Job description
We believe that the way people interact with their finances will drastically improve in the next few years. We’re dedicated to empowering this transformation by building the tools and experiences that thousands of developers use to create their own products. Plaid powers the tools millions of people rely on to live a healthier financial life. We work with thousands of companies like Venmo, SoFi, several of the Fortune 500, and many of the largest banks to make it easy for people to connect their financial accounts to the apps and services they want to use. Plaid’s network covers 12,000 financial institutions across the US, Canada, UK and Europe. Founded in 2013, the company is headquartered in San Francisco with offices in New York, Washington D.C., London and Amsterdam.



Role Description

We are seeking a Strategic Finance Lead to serve as a key partner to Plaid’s Engineering and Product  organization. This person will own product-level revenue and gross margin forecasting, as well as the analytical frameworks that guide how Plaid deploys capital and allocates resources across these areas.



As the Strategic Finance Lead, Product & Engineering, you will lead the finance partnership for a set of critical product areas, working directly with the relevant Product and Engineering leaders.



Responsibilities

 - Product-Level Forecasting. Own revenue and gross margin forecasts for your product areas, balancing bottom-up builds with top-down sanity checks to arrive at the most robust point of view.

 - Resource Allocation & Capacity Planning. Partner with Product and Engineering leaders on headcount planning, roadmap-based capacity forecasting, and OpEx discipline, surfacing and supporting the resolution of trade-offs.

 - Performance Visibility. Own the recurring reporting cadence for your product areas, including KPI tracking, variance analysis, and the early identification of risks and opportunities.

 - Cross-Functional Partnership. Serve as the primary finance partner to Product and Engineering leaders in your areas, and coordinate with GTM Finance on go-to-market approach, targets and economics.

 - Board & Executive Materials. Contribute analysis and narrative to the quarterly Board deck and ad hoc strategic reviews.

 - Mentorship. Mentor junior members of the Strategic Finance team in financial modeling, analytical rigor, and stakeholder management.



Qualifications

 - 8+ years of experience in FP&A, strategic finance, management consulting, investment banking, or private equity, including direct exposure to product or engineering finance at a high-growth technology company.

 - Strong command of product P&L mechanics, including revenue and gross margin forecasting, headcount and capacity planning, and software unit economics.

 - Comfortable owning ambiguous, judgment-intensive problems with minimal oversight, while exercising sound judgment about when to escalate.

 - Excellent written and verbal communication skills, with the ability to distill complex analysis into a clear narrative for Product, Engineering, and executive audiences.

 - A natural mentor who is energized by developing junior team members, even without a formal reporting line.

 - Experience partnering directly with Product or Engineering leaders is strongly preferred. Fintech or financial services experience is a plus but not required.



Our mission at Plaid is to unlock financial freedom for everyone. To support that mission, we seek to build a diverse team of driven individuals who care deeply about making the financial ecosystem more equitable. We recognize that strong qualifications can come from both prior work experiences and lived experiences. Job description
Our Purpose
Mastercard powers economies and empowers people in 200+ countries and territories worldwide. Together with our customers, we’re helping build a sustainable economy where everyone can prosper. We support a wide range of digital payments choices, making transactions secure, simple, smart and accessible. Title and Summary
Vice President, Finance Business Partner, Core Payments, Blockchain & Digital Currencies
Vice President, Finance Business Partner I Core Payments: Blockchain & Digital Currencies
Overview:
The Vice President, Finance Business Partner -- Blockchain & Digital Currencies at Mastercard will serve as the senior finance leader and strategic business partner for Digital Assets & Blockchain, providing executive financial leadership across business strategy, growth priorities, finance integration, planning, forecasting, performance management, investment prioritization, and decision support. This role will also oversee the finance integration agenda for strategic transactions, partnerships, and new business initiatives, including integration planning, close and Day 1 readiness, post-close execution, synergy and cost tracking, governance routines, operating model alignment, financial reporting readiness, control considerations, and cross-functional issue resolution. This role will partner closely with product, engineering, regional, corporate finance, integration management, legal, compliance, HR, sourcing, controllership, tax, treasury and business teams to ensure financial outcomes are aligned with organizational goals, governance expectations, and Mastercard’s broader strategy.
The ideal candidate is a strategic finance executive with strong business judgment, executive presence, integration experience, and the ability to lead through ambiguity in a fast-paced and rapidly evolving environment. This candidate will lead a team responsible for core finance business partnering and finance integration execution across Blockchain & Digital Currencies, translating complex business, operational, technology, regulatory, and integration needs into clear financial insights, executable plans, and executive-ready recommendations.
Role:
•	Lead the Finance Business Partner agenda for Blockchain & Digital Currencies, serving as the senior finance advisor to Digital Assets & Blockchain product, engineering and business leaders
•	Oversee budgeting, forecasting, and long-range planning processes across revenues, expenses, investments, personnel, vendor spend, integration costs, synergies, and macroeconomic drivers, ensuring plans are grounded in business drivers, strategic priorities, and financial targets
•	Own executive-level financial performance management, including month-end and quarter-end close review, forecast variance analysis, risks and opportunities, operating reviews, corporate reporting deliverables, and leadership narratives
•	Set the finance strategy and operating cadence for Blockchain & Digital Currencies, including KPIs, financial frameworks, reporting routines, governance forums, resource allocation, and decision support standards
•	Lead finance integration strategy, governance, and senior stakeholder engagement for strategic transactions, partnerships, and business initiatives across Digital Assets & Blockchain, including close readiness, Day 1 requirements, post-close milestones, operating model decisions, and escalation routines
•	Provide executive sponsorship and direction for finance integration workstreams across FP&A, Controllership, Tax, Treasury, Procurement, HR, Legal, Compliance, Sourcing, Integration Management and business teams, ensuring dependencies are identified, tracked, and resolved
•	Partner with senior business leaders to evaluate new products, partnerships, strategic investments, business cases, pricing, vendor negotiations, partner economics, operating model options, and return expectations
•	Proactively manage financial, operational, control, reporting, close, execution, regulatory, and market risks across the portfolio; develop mitigation plans and escalate key issues as needed
•	Develop and present executive-ready materials, including operating reviews, leadership updates, steering committee materials, finance readiness assessments, decision papers, performance narratives, and ad hoc strategic analysis
•	Identify and lead process improvement opportunities that strengthen financial discipline, enhance forecast accuracy, improve decision support, standardize integration routines, and increase operating efficiency
•	Lead, coach, and develop the Blockchain & Digital Currencies finance team, including the Director, Finance; Director, Finance Integration Lead; and Manager, Finance Integration, setting priorities, ensuring clear ownership, and fostering a high-performing, collaborative team environment
All About you:
•	Proven ability to define problems, collect data, establish facts, and draw valid conclusions 
•	Strong proficiency in financial planning and analysis, corporate finance and accounting 
•	Excellent analytical and problem-solving skills; highly proficient in Excel and PowerPoint 
•	Excellent verbal and written communication skills
•	Strong project management skills including superior organization, planning, and follow through. 
•	Able to foster a positive team environment and collaborate to produce outstanding results 
•	Able to work independently, prioritize effectively and drive process improvement initiatives 
•	Good knowledge of Business Intelligence tools and experience with financial systems/applications (e.g., Hyperion and Oracle) preferred
•	Advanced degree or MBA preferred.

The Director, Finance -- Blockchain & Digital Currencies at Mastercard will serve as the lead finance advisor to Digital Assets & Blockchain product and engineering leaders, helping shape business strategy, drive disciplined growth, and ensure robust financial management. This role will provide strategic financial leadership across planning, forecasting, performance management, investment prioritization, and executive decision support, ensuring financial outcomes are aligned with organizational goals.

This role will partner closely with product, engineering, regional, and corporate finance teams to assess the financial implications of business decisions and enable data-driven execution against Mastercard’s broader strategy.

Job description
SUMMARY

We are seeking a highly analytical, execution-oriented Finance Director to be based in San Francisco, CA and to own revenue forecasting and financial modeling for a consumption-based, high-growth business. This role is critical to how we understand, predict, and scale revenue in a usage-driven model where accuracy, iteration speed, and judgment under uncertainty matter.

This role will work in close, ongoing partnership with a dedicated Data Scientist focused on consumption forecasting, combining financial judgment and GTM context with statistical modeling, usage data, and predictive analytics. Together, you will build, test, and evolve the company’s core consumption and revenue forecasting frameworks.

The person in this seat must be deeply hands-on in Excel / Google Sheets, capable of building and rebuilding complex forecasting models, and comfortable iterating daily as new data, usage patterns, and GTM motions evolve.

The ideal candidate has early career training in investment banking, private equity / venture capital, or revenue-focused FP&A, followed by experience supporting a technology startup during its hypergrowth period, ideally with a consumption or usage-based revenue model.

You will partner closely with GTM leadership, RevOps, Data, Product, Accounting, and consumption forecasting Data Science to drive forecasting accuracy, revenue predictability, and decision-making at scale.


WHAT YOU’LL DO

Revenue Forecasting in a Consumption Model

 - Own the end-to-end revenue forecast for consumption-based products, including short-term (weekly/monthly) and long-range (quarterly/annual) views.

 - Build and maintain bottoms-up forecasting models that incorporate usage drivers, customer cohorts, ramp curves, contract structures (committed vs. PAYG), and expansion behavior.

 - Partner closely with Data Science to align financial forecasts with statistical consumption models, usage signals, and predictive outputs.

 - Continuously refine forecasts based on actuals, usage trends, pipeline changes, and GTM performance - iteration speed and accuracy are core expectations.

Deeply Hands-On With Financial Modeling

 - Develop and maintain highly detailed financial models in Google Sheets and Excel; this role requires expert-level proficiency.

 - Translate outputs from data science models into clear financial assumptions, scenarios, and executive-ready forecasts.

 - Perform scenario analysis, sensitivity analysis, and stress-testing to inform pricing, packaging, and GTM strategy.

 - Translate ambiguous or incomplete data into structured assumptions and clearly documented models.

Partner Closely With GTM, RevOps, and Data

 - Work directly with Sales, RevOps, and GTM leadership to connect pipeline, bookings, usage, and revenue outcomes.

 - Collaborate tightly with Data Science to evaluate forecast accuracy, diagnose variance, and improve model signal over time.

 - Analyze ARR vs. consumption dynamics, net dollar retention, expansion behavior, and usage efficiency by segment and customer cohort.

 - Support forecasting for new GTM motions, pricing changes, and product launches.

 - Lead headcount and tooling investment planning, partnering with functional leaders to translate growth plans into hiring roadmaps, tooling needs, and expense forecasts

Operate Effectively in a Hypergrowth Environment

 - Support a business that is scaling rapidly, where historical data is imperfect and judgment matters.

 - Help design forecasting processes that combine data-driven rigor with financial intuition and can scale over time.

 - Prepare materials and insights for QBRs, executive reviews, and board discussions, with a focus on revenue predictability, risk, and forecast confidence.

Cross-Functional Financial Leadership

 - Partner with Accounting on revenue recognition considerations and variance analysis tied to usage-based billing.

 - Support annual planning, rolling forecasts, and budget updates with a heavy emphasis on revenue accuracy.

 - Proactively identify risks and opportunities in the revenue model and communicate them clearly to leadership.


WHAT YOU’LL BRING

Background & Experience

 - 8-10 years of relevant experience, with early career training in one or more of the following:
   
   - Investment Banking
   
   - Private Equity or Venture Capital
   
   - Revenue-focused FP&A or Strategic Finance at a high-growth technology company

 - Direct experience forecasting consumption-based or usage-driven revenue is strongly preferred.

 - Experience operating in a hypergrowth environment (rapid customer growth, evolving GTM motion, limited historical data).

Technical & Analytical Skills

 - Expert-level Excel and Google Sheets skills; must be able to independently build complex, multi-layered financial models.

 - Strong understanding of revenue forecasting mechanics, cohort analysis, ramp modeling, and expansion dynamics.

 - Comfort partnering closely with Data Science and Analytics teams, translating model outputs into financial forecasts and decisions.

 - Ability to evaluate model performance, understand drivers of forecast error, and iterate assumptions accordingly.

Execution & Mindset

 - Highly iterative and action-oriented; comfortable revising forecasts frequently as new information becomes available.

 - Strong judgment under uncertainty; able to make and defend assumptions when data is incomplete or signals conflict.

 - Direct, clear communicator who can explain complex financial concepts to non-finance stakeholders.

 - High ownership mentality with a bias toward accountability and results.


NICE TO HAVE

 - Experience with SaaS and cloud metrics (NDR, usage efficiency, margin by product, CAC payback, LTV).

 - Familiarity with tools such as NetSuite, Salesforce, Snowflake, or modern BI platforms.

 - Prior experience working closely with Data Science teams on forecasting or predictive modeling.

 - MBA or CFA is preferred, but not required


COMPENSATION

 - The estimated pay range for this role is $236,500-$295,000 plus an annual bonus.

 - Additionally, this role is eligible to participate in Temporal’s equity plan.

Who we are

Samsara (NYSE: IOT) is the pioneer of the Connected Operations™ Cloud, which is a platform that enables organizations that depend on physical operations to harness Internet of Things (IoT) data to develop actionable insights and improve their operations. At Samsara, we are helping improve the safety, efficiency and sustainability of the physical operations that power our global economy. Representing more than 40% of global GDP, these industries are the infrastructure of our planet, including agriculture, construction, field services, transportation, and manufacturing — and we are excited to help digitally transform their operations at scale.

Working at Samsara means you’ll help define the future of physical operations and be on a team that’s shaping an exciting array of product solutions, including Video-Based Safety, Vehicle Telematics, Apps and Driver Workflows, and Equipment Monitoring. As part of a recently public company, you’ll have the autonomy and support to make an impact as we build for the long term.

Samsara is looking for a Senior Manager, Finance and Strategy, to partner with our Research & Development (R&D) organization. Samsara’s R&D organization creates the industry-leading IoT products and features that power our customer experience. Title and Summary
Manager, Financial Planning & Analysis
Overview
We are seeking a key contributor to join the Americas Financial Planning & Analysis (FP&A) team—supporting both the North America (NAM) and Latin America (LAC) regions. This role reports to the Director, Finance Business Partner, and will focus on supporting the Commercial Products business. The successful candidate will play a critical role in managing end-to-end financial processes, including budgeting, forecasting, performance reviews, variance analysis, reporting, and ad hoc financial analysis.
This role serves as the primary finance liaison for financial results, working closely with internal stakeholders to provide insights and explanations related to financial performance. You will be aligned to senior product executives and support strategic and operational decision-making across planning, forecasting, business development, and revenue optimization.
As a Finance Manager, you will act as a strategic business partner to a product segment, engaging directly with both the product team and the broader finance organization to help shape Mastercard’s Commercial Product performance, strategy, and long-term financial plan.
Can you craft a compelling narrative around the numbers?
Do you want to drive value as part of a customer-focused team?
Are you a strong communicator with the ability to influence stakeholders through insight and thought leadership?
Role Responsibilities
- Serve as the day-to-day finance business partner to a Commercial Product segment within the Americas region, working closely with internal product leaders and cross-functional teams.
- Lead and participate in financial discussions with internal stakeholders during periodic business and financial reviews.
- Manage operating expenses and contribute to the preparation of Quarterly Business Review (QBR) materials.
- Produce recurring and ad hoc financial deliverables for Product teams, Global Finance, and other key stakeholders.
- Drive the FP&A cycle including budgeting, forecasting, and performance tracking of volumes, revenue, product P&Ls, and expenses.
- Support new business initiatives, customer proposals, and go-to-market strategies through financial modeling and scenario analysis.
- Contribute financial inputs to goal setting and compensation planning as part of the annual planning process.
- Ensure financial alignment with both market-level priorities and Mastercard’s broader corporate objectives.
- Engage with customers when required to provide financial insight or support strategic initiatives.

Position Overview
Apollo is a high-growth, global alternative asset manager. In our asset management business, we seek to provide our clients excess return at every point along the risk-reward spectrum from investment grade to private equity with a focus on three investing strategies: yield, hybrid, and equity. For more than three decades, our investing expertise across our fully integrated platform has served the financial return needs of our clients and provided businesses with innovative capital solutions for growth. Through Athene, our retirement services business, we specialize in helping clients achieve financial security by providing a suite of retirement savings products and acting as a solutions provider to institutions. Our patient, creative, and knowledgeable approach to investing aligns our clients, businesses we invest in, our employees, and the communities we impact, to expand opportunity and achieve positive outcomes.
Come to Apollo and join a community of extraordinary people who are committed to a culture of excellence and collaboration in everything that we do. We invest in our people for the long term and are committed to supporting their development at every stage of their career.
The Strategic Finance team is seeking an Associate Director to join our team supporting coverage of the Principal Investing segment. Strategic Finance is responsible for the firm’s financial modeling, budgeting, forecasting, and other strategic financial analyses.  The role provides an opportunity to get involved in all these aspects and offers a unique vantage point of Apollo’s entire eco-system.  
Primary Responsibilities
Apollo Business Reporting, Budgeting and Forecasting

Financial Analytics / Strategic Initiatives and Transaction Support

Enhance financial models to fully capture business drivers and understand key trends, risks, and opportunities
Qualifications & Experience
5+ years of financial analysis experience, ideally in Investment Banking or Asset Management, in core CFO or FP&A functions, Big-4 valuation or advisory services

Passion for constant learning and developing an understanding beyond the pure mechanics of forecasting, strong appetite to always understand the “Bigger picture” including market dynamics and to question outcomes

Job description
About the Team

OpenAI’s Strategic Finance organization provides the financial insight and guidance that support the company’s long-term strategy and ambitious growth. We partner across Product, Partnerships, Engineering, and GTM to allocate resources to the highest-impact opportunities while preserving sustainable unit economics.

The Applications Technology Strategic Finance team works closely with Product and Engineering to scale core offerings and bring new products to market. We lead revenue forecasting and performance management across the product portfolio, and partner closely with the Infrastructure Engineering team to forecast infrastructure and compute demand and to manage margin targets and headcount planning with rigor.

About the Role

We are hiring a Director of Product Finance, Infrastructure and Compute Demand to help drive strategic decision-making across our product organization. This person will serve as the primary finance partner to the AGI Deployment (Applications) Infrastructure Engineering organization and play a critical role in connecting product usage, compute demand, infrastructure costs, and margin outcomes across the portfolio.

You will help build the frameworks that translate technical decisions into economic outcomes, shape planning across product and infrastructure teams, and ensure OpenAI is scaling its products with rigor and efficiency. You will also manage a small team, providing mentorship and leadership while remaining hands-on with analysis and execution.

This role is ideally based in our San Francisco HQ, but we are open to NYC and Seattle. We use a hybrid work model of 3 days in the office per week and offer relocation assistance to new employees.

In this role, you will:

 - Serve as the primary finance partner to the AGI Deployment (Applications) Infrastructure Engineering organization.

 - Own the consolidated compute and infrastructure demand forecast for the products we bring to market, partnering closely with capacity planning and supply teams to inform compute planning and allocation decisions.

 - Develop clear insight into the tradeoffs between customer experience, growth, and profitability, including how latency, throughput, and token usage affect margin performance.

 - Develop frameworks to evaluate ROI on compute and infrastructure spend across products, features, and user segments, and use those insights to inform investment and optimization decisions.

 - Create a durable cross-functional forum for compute economics, bringing together product, infrastructure, capacity planning, and finance to inform allocation, utilization, and margin tradeoffs.

 - Prepare high-quality materials for executive and Board-level discussions.

You might thrive in this role if you have:

 - 12+ years of experience in investment banking, consulting, or buy-side investing coupled with operational experience at a fast-paced, scaling company.

 - A strong ability to critically evaluate opportunities and risks in financials.

 - The ability to distill complex financial data into actionable insights and effectively communicate with leadership.

 - Excellent communication skills and the ability to craft compelling narratives using data.

 - Attention to detail and a commitment to accuracy in financial reporting and analysis.

 - Familiarity with business models and metrics relevant to OpenAI.

 - Enthusiasm for technology and artificial intelligence.

 - Extensively used AI.

This is a unique opportunity to influence the financial trajectory of cutting-edge AI products while working at the intersection of strategy, finance, and technology. If you’re passionate about driving growth and optimizing product performance, we’d love to hear from you!

About the Role

We are seeking a highly strategic and analytical Senior Manager, FP&A to join our growing finance organization at a fast-paced SaaS startup. This role will serve as a key business partner to Sales Leadership and will own critical areas across Sales Finance, commission planning, forecasting, and financial systems support. The ideal candidate brings deep FP&A expertise combined with hands-on experience supporting go-to-market organizations in high-growth SaaS environments.

You will play a critical role in driving forecasting accuracy, optimizing commission processes, improving financial planning infrastructure, and delivering actionable insights that support executive decision-making. This is a highly cross-functional role that partners closely with Finance, Revenue Operations, Sales Leadership, HR, and Accounting.

What You'll Do

Commission Operations & Compensation Integrity

Own the calculation, validation, and analysis of monthly commission payments

Ensure accuracy and integrity across compensation data and commission plans

Investigate discrepancies and resolve compensation-related issues in a timely, well-documented manner

Provide clear, professional support to sales teams on compensation questions, acting as a trusted point of contact

Support the execution and ongoing improvement of commission plans, including plan design feedback and process refinement

Commission Finance & Systems (Anaplan)

Lead commission finance activities, including commission modeling, compensation analytics, and sales commission calculations — all of which are currently built and operated in Anaplan

Own and maintain the Commissions module in Anaplan, ensuring accuracy, scalability, and proper documentation of processes

Support the design, maintenance, and optimization of FP&A systems and planning tools, with a primary focus on Anaplan

Sales Finance & Forecasting

Partner closely with Sales and GTM leadership to support strategic decision-making through financial modeling, forecasting, and performance analysis

Own and manage sales finance processes, including bookings forecasting, pipeline analysis, headcount planning, and productivity reporting

Manage and enhance forecasting processes across the commercial organization to improve predictability and operational visibility

Build scalable financial models to support annual planning, long-range planning, and scenario analysis

Analyze performance data to identify trends, risks, and opportunities across the sales organization

Cross-Functional Partnership & Reporting

Partner with Sales, Finance, and Operations teams globally to ensure consistent, accurate compensation and forecasting processes

Collaborate cross-functionally with Revenue Operations, Accounting, HR, and business leaders to ensure alignment across planning initiatives

Identify opportunities to improve processes, automate workflows, and increase operational efficiency within FP&A

Present financial insights and recommendations to senior leadership with a high degree of accuracy and attention to detail

 

What We're Looking For

7–12+ years of experience in FP&A, Sales Finance, Commercial Finance, or related finance functions

Prior experience supporting Sales or Go-to-Market organizations within a SaaS or high-growth technology company

Strong background in forecasting, budgeting, financial modeling, and commission finance

Hands-on Anaplan experience required — including direct experience building or managing commissions and forecasting models within Anaplan

Experience managing or supporting sales compensation planning, commission calculations, and payment validation

Strong understanding of SaaS business models and key performance metrics

Exceptional attention to detail and strong analytical problem-solving skills

Proven ability to work cross-functionally and influence stakeholders across multiple departments, including global teams

Strong communication and presentation skills, with the ability to simplify complex financial concepts for non-finance audiences

Experience in a startup or fast-scaling environment strongly preferred

Bachelor's degree in Finance, Accounting, Economics, or related field; MBA or advanced degree is a plus

Job description
Affirm is reinventing credit to make it more honest and friendly, giving consumers the flexibility to buy now and pay later without any hidden fees or compounding interest.
About the Finance team 

The Finance team ensures Affirm remains financially sound and strategically positioned for growth. Our team manages financial planning, accounting, pricing, vendor management, tax, investor relations, and corporate development. We deliver timely insights, accurate reporting, and careful analysis to support decision-making. From day-to-day financial operations to major investments, we enable sustainable, informed growth by maintaining strong fiscal discipline.

About the role

We are looking for a self-motivated and resourceful Bank Head of FP&A to join us during a pivotal moment of growth. As Affirm Bank is currently in its formation phase, we are actively building out our foundational team. Reporting to the Bank CFO, the Bank Head of FP&A will be central to this effort, playing a key role in the formation and operational build-out of the Bank.

The Bank Head of FP&A will play a critical role in building the team and leading day-to-day finance operations. Core responsibilities include managing the forecasting and budget process, developing robust financial models, capital stress testing and monitoring, financial analysis, and partnering with Affirm, Inc. cross-functional teams on financial workflows.

What you'll do

Team Leadership & Talent Development: 
Build and lead the Bank strategic finance team, providing strategic direction, recruitment, and coaching as we establish the function.

Financial Planning & Analysis:
 Lead the end-to-end forecasting and budget process, ensuring alignment with organizational goals during this critical startup phase. Perform rigorous financial analysis to inform strategic decision-making.

Financial Modeling:
 Develop and maintain complex financial models to support strategic planning, scenario analysis, and forecasting for the Bank.

Capital Strategy:
 Oversee capital stress testing and ongoing monitoring. Partner cross-functionally to validate inputs, pressure-test key drivers, and ensure outputs reflect current business dynamics.

Performance Management & Reporting:
 Monitor performance against forecasts, diagnose variances, identify root causes, and provide forward-looking insights to drive action.

Strategic Decision Support:
 Provide decision support on strategic initiatives, evaluating trade-offs and P&L implications. Translate complex analyses into clear, executive-ready narratives that drive insight, inform strategy, and align stakeholders.

Cross-Functional Partnership:
 Partner closely with Affirm, Inc. cross-functional teams to integrate financial workflows.

What we look for

Minimum 8+ years relevant experience in analytical roles (e.g., strategic finance, consulting, data science, or analytics), with demonstrated impact on business outcomes

Minimum 5 years of people management experience

Proven ability to operate with ownership and influence in a fast-paced, ambiguous environment, with strong critical thinking and problem-solving skills

Strong business judgment, with the ability to connect detailed analyses to broader strategic context and executive-level decisions

Experience partnering with senior stakeholders and influencing decisions across cross-functional teams

Financial modeling proficiency; working knowledge of modeling tools and experience with data visualization tools preferred

Familiarity with fintech, lending and payments business model preferred

Planning Systems & Financial Models: You’ll build stuff. Partner with the IT, People, & Finance  teams to help modernize/automate our forecasting and planning tools. People Management: You’ll lead a team. To do all the above well, you’ll manage, mentor, and develop a small team (but high-impact) team of analysts and associates and help create/shape a world-class Corp FP&A team

Forecast global budget items such as payroll taxes, benefits and allocations  

What You'll Need:

Bachelor’s Degree; finance, business or economics preferred, MBA a plus

5+ years of experience within FP&A or other finance functions

Job description
Our Mission

Healthcare should work for patients, but it doesn’t. In their time of need, they call down outdated insurance directories. Then wait on hold. Then wait weeks for the privilege of a visit. Then wait in a room solely designed for waiting. Then wait for a surprise bill. In any other consumer industry, the companies delivering such a poor customer experience would not survive. But in healthcare, patients lack market power. Which means they are expected to accept the unacceptable.

 

Zocdoc’s mission is to give power to the patient. To do that, we’ve built the leading healthcare marketplace that makes it easy to find and book in-person or virtual care in all 50 states, across +200 specialties and +12k insurance plans. By giving patients the ability to see and choose, we give them power. In doing so, we can make healthcare work like every other consumer sector, where businesses compete for customers, not the other way around. In time, this will drive quality up and prices down. 

 

We’re 18 years old and the leader in our space, but we are still just getting started. If you like solving important, complex problems alongside deeply thoughtful, driven, and collaborative teammates, read on.

 
Your impact on our mission

We are looking for a highly motivated and analytical Senior Manager, FP&A to support Zocdoc’s R&D and operations teams. In this role, you will use financial and operational insights to guide investment, headcount, and performance decisions. You will partner closely with functional leadership to support strategic planning, resource allocation, and operating efficiency. This role offers an exciting opportunity to shape decision-making in key parts of the business and play a pivotal role in driving financial performance and long-term success.

Improve planning processes, team workflows, and reporting approaches to increase consistency, efficiency, and decision usefulness

An overview of this role

GitLab is looking for a Manager, Strategic Finance to join our Strategic Finance team. This role focuses on financial planning, analysis, and business operations. You’ll lead modeling for long-range bookings forecasts and help shape earnings guidance and recommendations for GitLab executives and the Board of Directors. Reporting to a Director, you’ll work closely with the Chief Financial Officer, executive leaders, and partners across Product, Sales, Marketing, Revenue Operations, and Engineering.

What You’ll Do
  

Own and improve demand-based models for Annual Recurring Revenue (ARR) growth and bookings, including seat, price, tier, customer retention, and consumption dynamics across GitLab’s products.

Manage the long-range financial model for bookings by product and forecast new business ARR and customer growth by geography and segment.

Develop new business targets and assessments that support Marketing, Sales Development Representatives, Account Executives, and Partners.

Build and govern business cases for new product offerings, including artificial intelligence products and strategic partnerships, in collaboration with Finance, Product, Sales Strategy, Marketing, and Engineering.

Support pricing and packaging strategy through reporting, price-change assessment, and scenario modeling.

Assess GitLab’s market opportunity and market landscape, assess why GitLab wins and loses deals, and link customer demand to product and growth priorities.

Assess unit economics across customer segments and geographies, including lifetime value, customer acquisition cost, payback periods, marketing return on investment, and gross margin by product line.

Advise on strategic deals and partnerships, assess product key performance indicators, and explain how adoption trends may affect bookings and future financial performance.

What You’ll Bring 

Experience in strategic finance, investment banking, private equity, venture capital, hedge funds, management consulting, a technology startup, or a similarly analytical role.

A strategic thinker with broad business acumen and a passion for software and AI technologies and their commercialization

Self starter with an ownership mentality who thrives in ambiguity and delivers insights to executives in a thoughtful and persuasive manner

Strong financial modeling skills and experience building new models and processes from scratch, improving existing ones, and structuring ambiguous business questions from fundamentals.

Experience translating complex data into clear, polished recommendations for executive audiences.

Define problems, manage multiple time-sensitive projects, and deliver rigorous work with limited direction.

Build cross-functional relationships and work effectively through high-stakes or changing priorities.

Job description
The Role
We are seeking a Principal Finance Manager to lead Inventory and Capacity Planning Finance. This role owns the financial strategy and controllership for the inventory and capacity planning systems supporting SCOT's Long Term Planning and Forecasting business team — connecting system decisions to financial outcomes, building mechanisms for emerging planning systems, and reporting to senior finance leadership on inventory Working Capital and Free Cash Flow performance.
You will serve as the cross-functional bridge between SCOT systems, Ops and Finance. You will own inventory Working Capital and Free Cash Flow financial planning and reporting across OP, Guidance, and Month End Close cycles. You will also establish controllership for the new SCOT systems being developed to plan inventory, cube, and capacity and shape mechanisms from the ground up as these systems come online. We seek a leader who thrives in ambiguity, brings financial rigor to complex autonomous systems, and is energized by building new frameworks where none exist today.
Key job responsibilities
- Set the strategic direction for Inventory and Capacity Planning Finance, establishing mechanisms to drive controllership of inventory, cube, and capacity planning systems
- Own Inventory Working Capital and Free Cash Flow financial planning and reporting for OP, Guidance, and Month End Close cycles.
- Connect system decisions to financial outcomes, building feedback loops that provide leadership visibility and drive continuous improvement
- Serve as the cross-functional bridge between SCOT systems, Ops, and Finance — creating operational discipline for financial planning inputs
- Establish controllership and audit mechanisms for new SCOT planning systems as they are developed and deployed
- Engage in system design as the financial subject matter expert, ensuring planning algorithms incorporate appropriate financial inputs
- Champion AI adoption and leverage AI tools to accelerate analysis, continuously exploring new ways to deliver insights faster
About the team
The Supply Chain Optimization Technology (SCOT) team sits at the core of Amazon's global supply chain — determining what inventory to buy, when to buy it, how much to order, and where to position it to delight customers while meeting Amazon's business objectives. Operating at massive scale across hundreds of product lines and billions of dollars of worldwide inventory, SCOT's fully in-house, automated planning and optimization systems are among the most sophisticated in the world. Finance within SCOT is a high-impact function, partnering directly with AI and machine learning engineers, scientists, product managers, and operations leaders to shape strategy, drive investment decisions, and deliver measurable improvements to Amazon's supply chain economics.

- 10+ years of tax, finance or a related analytical field experience
- 10+ years of dissecting financial data and identifying patterns that support business strategy experience
- 7+ years of finance technology, forecast & budgeting, or other finance-focused competency experience
- Experience with Tableau, Python, SQB, Quicksight, Cognos or or other BI packages
- Experience using complex financial models, KPIs, and data analysis to inform strategic business decisions and influence cross-functional stakeholders, with proven business impact (e.g., financial savings, operational improvements, or customer benefits)
- Experience with budgeting, forecasting and variance analysis
- Bachelor's degree in finance, accounting or related field

- MBA, or CPA
- Experience in a leadership position managing finance, accounting and operational processes

Job description
ABOUT THE ROLE

The Senior Manager, Financial Systems & Reporting Transformation is responsible for leading the redesign of the company's financial master data — including the chart of accounts, cost centers, profit centers, product hierarchies, and dimensional reporting structures — with the objective of enhancing profitability insights, standardizing management reporting, and enabling seamless upstream consolidation with the parent company. 

YOUR DAILY IMPACT AT PELOTON

Lead the end-to-end redesign of the company's financial master data structures — chart of accounts, cost centers, profit centers, functional areas, internal orders, product hierarchies, customer/vendor master, and reporting dimensions — to enable enhanced profitability, channel, and product-level reporting

Define the target-state data model required for seamless consolidation with the parent company's reporting environment, including alignment of GL accounts, entity structures, intercompany conventions, and dimensional hierarchies

Develop and execute the migration plan from the legacy fragmented environment to the redesigned master data model, including data mapping, cleansing, cutover, and validation

Establish master data governance (ownership, request/approval workflows, change controls, and audit trails) to preserve data integrity post-implementation

Partner with IT and the SAP functional teams to configure and validate master data changes in SAP ECC, ensuring downstream impacts to sub-ledgers, allocations, and reporting are fully understood and controlled

Spearhead process optimization efforts within key functional areas, including the month-end close process, consolidations, allocations, and monthly management reporting

Drive efficiency and process design improvements within major transaction cycles: Order to Cash (OTC), Procure to Pay (PTP), Inventory, and Fixed Assets — with particular focus on how master data drives accuracy, controls, and reporting granularity

Identify, champion, and execute opportunities to leverage IT solutions — SAP ECC configuration, reporting layers, and complementary tools — to create efficiencies and strengthen internal controls

Collaborate directly with global and regional business leaders to drive actionable roadmaps for process simplification and automation, ensuring solutions are pragmatic and scalable

Serve as the lead point of contact for financial data alignment with the parent company, ensuring the redesigned master data supports consolidated reporting, elimination entries, and management-view analytics

Partner with the parent company's controllership and FP&A teams to define mapping rules, submission cadences, and data quality standards

Support the design of the future-state consolidation process, including any tooling, mapping tables, or reporting bridges required to reconcile local and consolidated views

YOU BRING TO PELOTON

BA/BS degree in Accounting/Finance or related field

CPA preferred (or equivalent)

8+ years of relevant experience in accounting, finance transformation, or reporting at a public company and/or external audit of public companies. Public company experience is required

Hands-on SAP ECC experience is required, including a working knowledge of the FI/CO module, master data structures (chart of accounts, cost centers, profit centers, internal orders), and how master data drives sub-ledger and reporting outcomes

Demonstrated experience leading or contributing to a financial master data redesign, chart of accounts redesign, or ERP finance transformation program

Experience or strong familiarity with AI/LLM-enabled finance tools (e.g., Microsoft Copilot, ChatGPT/Claude for data analysis, or AI-driven reconciliation modules inside major ERPs/EPMs)

Experience supporting or enabling consolidations into a parent company's reporting environment, including intercompany, elimination, and mapping conventions

Previous participation in existing internal control programs; strong understanding of SOX implications of master data and system changes

Data analytics experience preferred; comfort working with large data sets and reporting tools to validate and communicate the impact of master data changes

Project management skills — effective consultation, interpersonal, prioritization of topics/issues, and organization. Experience leading cross-functional transformation workstreams is strongly preferred

Strong written and verbal communication skills, with the ability to translate technical data model decisions into business-relevant impacts

Must be a highly organized, detail-oriented self-starter with the ability to work independently and within a team environment

Ability to work effectively with all levels of an organization, from analyst to executive, and across accounting, IT, FP&A, and parent-company stakeholders

An objective, independent, mature, unbiased individual with the highest integrity

Superior problem-solving skills and ability to manage competing priorities and deadlines

The role:

We are looking for a highly motivated and experienced Finance Business Partner to join our growing Strategic Finance team. Strategic Finance shapes SoFi’s capital allocation strategy by assessing investment opportunities, framing key trade-offs, and prioritizing resources against the company’s most important growth objectives. As a key business partner, you will provide financial leadership and strategic insights to two of SoFi's most critical and rapidly growing business lines: SoFi Checking & Savings and SoFi Credit Card. SoFi is an AI-forward organization, and you will be expected to responsibly use our suite of AI tools to build and enhance financial models, accelerate analysis, automate workflows, and improve decision-making. 

What you’ll do:

Own the financial strategy, multi-year plan, annual operating plan, and rolling forecasts for SoFi’s rapidly growing Checking & Savings and Credit Card businesses

Serve as a strategic thought partner to Business Unit leaders, translating financial and operational data into insights and recommendations that shape growth strategy and business performance

Build and analyze customer and product-level unit economics including LTV, CAC, payback periods, and risk-adjusted returns across cohorts, acquisition channels, products, and member segments

Drive disciplined capital allocation through business cases, trade-off analyses, and post-investment performance measurement 

Partner with Product, Marketing, Risk, Operations, Treasury, Accounting, and Data teams to evaluate new products, pricing and rewards strategies, marketing programs, partnerships, and operating initiatives

Lead scenario planning and sensitivity analyses to assess the impact of changes in interest rates, member behavior, credit performance, competitive dynamics, and other key business drivers

Develop business cases for major initiatives and establish post-investment measurement processes to evaluate realized returns, surface learnings, and improve future capital-allocation decisions

Synthesize complex analyses into clear, compelling recommendations and communicate business performance, risks, and opportunities to Executive Leadership

Identify and scale high-impact AI use cases that expand Strategic Finance’s capabilities, capacity, and analytical rigor 

Lead, develop, and inspire a high-performing team while fostering strong cross-functional collaboration, accountability, inclusion, and mentorship within and outside of Finance

What you’ll need:

12+ years of experience in Strategic Finance, Corporate Finance, FP&A, investment banking, or a related field, ideally within fintech or consumer financial services

Deep expertise in consumer financial-services economics, with a strong command of Credit Card unit economics and P&L dynamics

Demonstrated expertise analyzing unit economics, including LTV/CAC, payback periods, and risk-adjusted returns, across cohorts, channels, products, and segments

Expert financial modeling and scenario-analysis skills, including the ability to build driver-based models from scratch and analyze large data sets

Fluency with AI tools and demonstrated ability to apply them to financial modeling, analysis, workflow automation, and decision support

Creative and strategic problem-solving skills, with the ability to bring structure to ambiguous business questions

Bachelor’s degree in Finance, Economics, Accounting, or a related field, or equivalent practical experience

Job description
North American Sort centers are part of the Amazon Transportation Services which enables next day free delivery for Prime members and seven days a week delivery for our customers. Sort Centers are part of the middle-mile operation; every day several million packages from multiple fulfillment centers across the US and Canada arrive in one of the ~100 sort centers where they are sorted to specific last mile delivery locations, in effect consolidating volumes going to the same place, which improves truck utilization and reduces transportation costs. Since launching operations in 2013, we have provided billions of dollars of savings to Amazon, improved shipping times for our customers and enabled delivery seven days a week - our growing presence in logistics space led Amazon to be one of the top 5 logistic operators in the US.
The North American Sort Center team is responsible for the Financial Planning and Analysis (FP&A), investment strategy and new transportation initiatives for this operation. This is an exciting opportunity to partner cross-functionally to ensure informed decision making, innovation and sustainability in processes for a growing network. Experience on this team opens up varying career opportunities across Amazon such as transitions to other business lines, project management roles, and/or management opportunities locally, regionally, and globally.

About the team

Own consolidated reporting across Top of Funnel, Conversion, Users, Volume, and Loan Mix; synthesize key drivers including mix, credit, pricing, and commercial impacts

Provide decision support on strategic initiatives, evaluating trade-offs and P&L implications to inform business and investment decisions

Job description
FP&A Manager, Sales & Marketing (M3, Player/Coach)
Role summary 

Lead a small FP&A team as a player/coach supporting a 10,000-person Sales & Marketing organization in North America.

Drive weekly forecast rigor, quarter-end close discipline, OPEX and headcount forecasting, and commission modeling/analysis.

Partner closely with senior executives (SVPs, EVPs, President level) to translate complex financial and operational concepts into concise, executive-ready insights and recommendations.

 

Key responsibilities

  Planning and forecasting 

Own OPEX and headcount planning, forecasting, and variance analysis; deliver weekly forecast updates and quarterly outlooks.

Run Annual Operating Plan/Long Range Forecasting Plan cycles; build driver-based models for bookings, pipeline health, productivity, program spend, and ROI.

Maintain controllership rigor: accruals, reclasses, prepaids, and month/quarter-end close activities with clear bridges (Plan vs. Forecast vs. Actuals).

Commissions

Partner with Finance business leaders on commission plan modeling, scenario analysis (tiers, accelerators, SPIFs), and financial impact to budgets and forecast.

Support audit and compliance requirements; ensure alignment with SOX and data governance controls.

Business partnership and insights 

Serve as primary finance partner to Sales and Marketing leadership; collaborate with Sales Ops/Bus Ops, Marketing Ops, HR, Accounting/Controllership, and Corporate FP&A.

Produce executive-ready narratives, dashboards, and readouts that distill complex topics into concise, decision-oriented insights.

Drive cross-functional initiatives to improve forecast accuracy, pipeline-to-revenue conversion insights, marketing ROI, and resource allocation.

Team leadership

Lead and develop a team of ~3 analysts; set clear goals, prioritize workload, and cultivate a culture of learning, accountability, and partnership.

Operate as a player/coach—personally building models, reviews, and executive materials while coaching the team to scale impact.

Establish standard work, SOPs, and calendarized cadences for weekly forecasts, MBRs/QBRs, and quarter-close deliverables.

Process, systems, and controls 

Standardize and automate end-to-end FP&A processes; streamline multi-step workflows and resolve cross-functional issues.

Champion data integrity, least-privilege access, and compliance with SOX and privacy requirements.

Leverage enterprise systems and BI tools to automate reporting and accelerate insights.

Job description
Amazon’s North American Customer Fulfillment (NACF) Finance is looking for a Finance Manager to be the trusted finance partner in the ARS (Amazon Robotics Sortable) Network. Amazon operates the most complex retail networks in the world, delivering billions of packages to hundreds of millions of homes and businesses annually. Come be part of this tremendous growth and join a dedicated team committed to operational and financial excellence where you will focus on the inputs, think like an owner and where every day is Day One. Our ARS Finance team closely partners across Operations and Program teams to support a rapidly expanding business while building a connected network of fulfillment centers, delivery stations, sort centers, vans, box trucks, and technology to deliver customers' packages on time - often next day, sometimes faster. Come make history with us.
Key job responsibilities
The ARS Finance team seeks a Finance leader to partner with the Operations, Supply Chain, Retail, and Strategy Teams, and to drive an agenda of optimization, efficiency, and insights across the US ARS Network. This is a unique opportunity be a leader and key business partner to oversee a vast network of fulfillment centers, coordinate initiatives that span across multiple organizations and stakeholders with high visibility to senior leadership. Experience in this team opens up several career opportunities across Amazon and access to making an impact to the broader end-to-end operational vertical.
The successful candidate will demonstrate the following:
- They influence business owners, support thoughtful decision-making, and adapt to a rapidly-evolving environment.
- They collaborate with executives and various business units across Amazon.
- They bring excellent verbal and written communication skills.
- They prioritize the most impactful projects and programs that will drive the ARS Fulfillment business segment forward. 
About the team
This role will partner closely with a broad range of Senior Leaders in Finance, Operations, Supply Chain, Engineering, Procurement, Accounting, HR, and Technology. This role plays a critical role as a financial advocate and business partner. This role will design, develop, and maintain performance measurements and reporting on strategic input drivers to continuously improve the economics of the business.

Job description
1 in 4 people in the US have a treatable mental health condition, but most providers don't accept insurance, making therapy too expensive for most people. Headway’s mission is to fix this by building a new mental healthcare system everyone can access. We started by solving the biggest barrier to care: insurance. The admin work - credentialing, claims, payment reconciliation - is a nightmare. We've automated that.



But we're going further. Over 75,000 providers across all 50 states run their practice on our software, serving over 1 million patients. We are building the best tools for therapists to run their entire practice, reimagining the experience of finding a therapist, and investing in the platform foundations to enable this at scale. We aren't just a billing layer; we are becoming the platform where care actually happens.



We're a Series D company with $325M+ in funding (a16z, Accel, Spark Capital, etc.), looking for exceptional people to help us achieve this mission. We want your time here to be the most meaningful experience of your career. Join us, and help change mental healthcare for the better.



Financial planning can sound like month-end close and rearview-mirror reporting. At Headway's stage, it's the opposite. As we scale toward $1B+ in revenue, FP&A is the engine that lets the company see itself clearly — where to invest and how to grow efficiently.

We're looking for an FP&A Manager who sees this not as a reporting function, but as one of the most operationally influential seats in the company. You'll partner directly with Finance leadership and department leaders across CX, R&D, People, and G&A — connecting the decisions those teams make on the ground to the company's broader financial picture, and driving the execution that turns plans into results. You won't just report the numbers; you'll shape the decisions behind them.

If you're an analytical, detail-oriented finance partner who wants real influence on how a high-growth company spends, plans, and scales — and you care about the mission behind the work — this role is built for you.

You will:

 - Be the financial thought partner and strategist for key parts of Headway's business. Serve as the embedded Finance partner to several major departments — owning the financial strategy behind how those teams invest, manage spend, and drive toward profitability. This is thought partnership, not just reporting: you'll surface tradeoffs, pressure-test plans, and help leaders make sharper financial decisions.

 - Lead financial deep dives that drive decisions. Run the financial analyses and deep dives that identify business problems, root-cause them, and surface early signals — then turn that into strategies that accelerate progress.

 - Help the company understand its performance at both a micro and macro level. Support company-wide consolidation, shape the performance narrative that tells leadership how the business is really tracking, and take on the ad-hoc financial analyses that inevitably arise in a fast-scaling business.

 - Help drive the company's strategic planning. Play a central role in the processes where Headway defines its strategy and understands the financial implications — Annual Planning, Long-Range Vision — serving as a key Finance voice to teams across the company.

 - Bring AI into the workflow. Actively apply AI tools to drive faster insight, greater efficiency, and smarter decision-making across the FP&A workflow — and help shape how the team works as those tools evolve.

You’d be a great fit if:

 - You've owned the numbers behind a fast-growing business — and made them mean something. You have 6+ years of experience, ideally blending Investment Banking, Consulting, or Private Equity/VC with a highly analytical strategic finance role at a fast-growing company. You bring a point of view on day one.

 - You take a "measure twice, cut once" approach to financial data. You're detail-oriented and rigorous, highly proficient in Google Sheets and BI tools, and comfortable working with large, complex datasets.

 - You make the business better, not just the model. You embed with department leaders, earn their trust quickly, and translate finance into decisions that sharpen spend discipline and efficiency.

 - You see AI as a competitive advantage for finance, not a feature to explore someday. You actively use AI tools to work faster, sharpen your analysis, and make smarter calls — and you bring that orientation to the team around you.

 - You bring structure to ambiguity. You're a resourceful self-starter who seeks context, solves open-ended problems, anticipates stakeholder needs, and stays flexible when priorities shift.

 - You communicate complex insights clearly. You tailor your message to the audience — finance vs. business and product partners — and make the takeaway impossible to miss.

Job description
The CRE Finance Banker is a senior-level position responsible for originating, structuring, underwriting, and executing commercial real estate financing transactions for Citi's institutional clients. The role works closely with senior bankers, product partners, investors, and sponsors to deliver innovative financing solutions across the commercial real estate capital structure, including mortgage loans, CMBS, mezzanine debt, preferred equity, and other structured finance products. The overall objective of this role is to manage client relationships, execute transactions, identify new business opportunities, and contribute to the growth and strategic development of the CRE Finance platform.
Responsibilities: 
Responsible for screening, sizing, structuring, underwriting, originating, and closing a broad range of real estate financing products including CMBS, mortgage loans, mezzanine/preferred debt, back leverage loans/facilities, acquisition financings, and take private financings across various real estate property sectors.
Assume significant responsibility across all phases of transaction execution, business development, client relationship management, and the overall growth of the CRE Finance franchise.
Underwrite real estate transactions through detailed cash flow analysis, collateral and sponsor evaluation, market assessments, valuation analysis, and risk examination.
Mentor and oversee a team of analysts and associates in the execution of CRE Finance transactions, providing guidance on underwriting, structuring, and deal execution.
Draft and review offering materials including pitch books, term sheets, offering circulars, rating agency presentations, debt offering memoranda, and internal committee memos.
Review and interpret third-party due diligence reports, including appraisals, engineering reports, environmental assessments, market studies, property leases, rent rolls, and historical and projected operating statements.
Appropriately assess risk when business decisions are made, demonstrating particular consideration for the firm's reputation and safeguarding Citigroup, its clients and their assets, by driving compliance with applicable laws, rules and regulations, adhering to Citi policies, applying sound ethical judgment regarding personal behavior, conduct and business practices, and escalating, managing and reporting control issues with transparency.
Qualifications:
5-8 years of experience
Degree in Finance or closely related areas of Business Administration preferred
Advanced financial modeling and valuation skills, including cash flow modeling, debt sizing, sensitivity analysis, and commercial real estate valuation methodologies.
Comprehensive knowledge of related products, pertinent regulations and lending and credit approval processes
Consistently demonstrates clear and concise written and verbal communication skills
Experience with large sponsor funds, corporate clients, capital markets products, finance and securities products.
Series 79, 7 and 63 preferred
Education:
Bachelor’s degree/University degree or equivalent experience
MBA or Master's Degree in Business preferred

Who we are

Samsara (NYSE: IOT) is the pioneer of the Connected Operations™ Cloud, which is a platform that enables organizations that depend on physical operations to harness Internet of Things (IoT) data to develop actionable insights and improve their operations. At Samsara, we are helping improve the safety, efficiency and sustainability of the physical operations that power our global economy. Representing more than 40% of global GDP, these industries are the infrastructure of our planet, including agriculture, construction, field services, transportation, and manufacturing — and we are excited to help digitally transform their operations at scale.

Working at Samsara means you’ll help define the future of physical operations and be on a team that’s shaping an exciting array of product solutions, including Video-Based Safety, Vehicle Telematics, Apps and Driver Workflows, and Equipment Monitoring. As part of a recently public company, you’ll have the autonomy and support to make an impact as we build for the long term.

Samsara is hiring a Sr Manager, Finance and Strategy to partner with our global Marketing department. Finance at Brex

We partner across teams at Brex to ensure our business is building and planning with the same dream big goals throughout our entire organization. We’re not here to report on outcomes; we shape them. What you’ll do

What you’ll do

Your work will directly impact our salesforce motivation and productivity, helping us achieve our growth targets. About this role

We’re looking for a high-agency Strategic Finance Lead to own financial and strategic analysis for Faire’s emerging fulfillment and logistics investments. You’ll drive mission-critical work end to end – from unit economics and scenario modeling to executive-ready narratives and partnership with Product, GTM, Business Development, and Operations. This role is central to how we scale fulfillment from zero to one: making disciplined tradeoffs on cost-to-serve, network economics, and partnerships so we improve service for brands and retailers while de-risking long-term financial outcomes.

Job description

Capital and operational efficiency both matter at Faire, but fulfillment is increasingly where scale, customer experience, and P&L intersect. The Finance & Strategy team helps ensure we invest with clarity – linking strategy to numbers and turning complexity into decisions that leadership can act on. As 
Strategic Finance Lead
, you will conduct the financial and strategic analysis that informs how Faire optimally invests in scaling and improving fulfillment and logistics. That includes deep scenario modeling and a firm grasp of the building blocks of our unit economics, so we can iterate confidently as the fulfillment operation grows.

This is an ideal role for someone who thrives in a dynamic, highly collaborative, data-driven environment and wants to work closely across functions - with outsized impact on how the marketplace delivers for customers.

What you’ll do

Own fulfillment unit economics and scenario modeling.
 Build and maintain the financial models that underpin Faire’s fulfillment strategy—cost-to-serve, network economics, carrier and 3PL pricing—and translate outputs into clear tradeoffs that inform investment decisions and operational priorities.

Support 3PL and carrier contract strategy.
 Lead financial analysis for negotiations, including scenario modeling across rate structures, volume commitments, and service tiers so Faire enters and renews contracts from a position of analytical strength.

Connect fulfillment to brand, retailer, and GMV outcomes.
 Develop a sharp view of how fulfillment cost, speed, and reliability intersect with brand and retailer behavior; use those insights to shape product and GTM strategy.

Influence the fulfillment roadmap at leadership level.
 Partner with Product, GTM, and Business Development on investment decisions; deliver hypothesis-driven analysis that ties operational tradeoffs to Faire’s broader financial outputs.

Elevate executive communication.
 Distill complex fulfillment economics into clear, decision-ready narratives—from board-level framing to operational deep dives—so the right detail reaches the right audience.

Qualifications

Bachelor’s degree and 5+ years in strategic corporate finance, private equity / venture capital, investment banking, management consulting, and/or finance/partnering roles with heavy operational or logistics-adjacent complexity

Strong modeling muscle: able to build and stress-test financial models that reflect operational reality, not just spreadsheet theory

Analytical rigor: synthesize information, decompose problems, and use data and logic to diagnose root causes—comfortable going deep on unit economics without losing the strategic question

Excellent communication: credible, structured storytelling for executives and cross-functional partners; able to zoom between detail and narrative

Cross-functional operator: track record building trust and alignment across Product, GTM, Business Development, and Operations; organized with strong deadline management across concurrent workstreams

Team-oriented: motivated by business outcomes over personal recognition in a high-impact IC role

Nice to have

Experience in e-commerce, marketplaces, or logistics-intensive businesses where fulfillment economics are central to the P&L

AI-forward working style
:
 Actively experiments with AI tools to accelerate and sharpen analytical work — whether that's automating rote modeling tasks, pressure-testing assumptions, or generating faster first drafts of complex analyses. 

Commercial acumen supporting vendor or carrier negotiations—modeling rate structures, volume commitments, and contract scenarios

SQL or comparable data proficiency to pull and manipulate analyses without relying on partners for every cut
